West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Open the window at Behind House, then go west into the Kitchen. This sequence recorded +10 in repeated episodes.
2. Take the egg from Up a Tree and 'put egg in case' in the Living Room before any descent. Banking it first was never achieved this episode.
3. Before leaving the Living Room for the cellar, take the lantern and light it. Moving the rug and opening the trap door led down, recorded +25.
4. In the Gallery, take the art, then try the unexplored exits (Studio and beyond), including 'up', before backtracking.
5. When a stagnation warning appears, try an untested exit or verb in the current room. Do not repeat the last two moves.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Never go north from the Cellar. Troll Room entries ended in a -10 death. Never use 'give' on the troll or 'hit troll'. Treat Maze 'take coins' as unreachable.
2. Do not descend the trap door with an unbanked treasure. It barred behind the Actor and no way home was ever verified.
3. Do not cycle Cellar/East Chasm/Gallery/Studio. About 65 steps passed here with zero new rooms or score; repeated edges mean choose a new exit.
4. Do not spend opening steps on mailbox, leaflet, sack, garlic or sword takes. None scored. Go straight to the egg and window.
5. Do not follow the 'return route' goal by retracing edges already confirmed dead. Test untried directions like 'up' and 'down' in each room instead.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

You are about 10 feet above the ground nestled among some large branches. The nearest branch above you is above your reach.

The egg appears extremely fragile.

Historian episode review — episode 50
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

Strongest decisions were early: the Actor took the egg and banked it ('put egg in case', +5 each recorded), went west through the open window (+10), went down the trap door (+25) and took the art (+4). That totals 49 before a final -10, ending at 39. The briefing's 'give troll painting' and 'never tried up' conflict with the logs. The last recorded action was 'north' from Gallery (-10, terminal), and 'up' from Studio did reach Kitchen, then Attic and Living Room, around step 379. The Actor cycled Cellar/East Chasm/Gallery/Studio for most of the episode with no new rooms or score. It also dropped and re-took lunch, pepper and manual, and followed the symbolic Maze 'take coins' goal about 20 times without questioning it. It found 'up' only very late, then went back underground instead of exploring above ground. Next time: try Studio 'up' right after the art, test banking the art, and explore new rooms.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Take the egg in Up a Tree and 'put egg in case' in the Living Room before descending; both recorded +5 here.
2. Open the window at Behind House, then go west into the Kitchen; this recorded +10 again.
3. In the Living Room, open the trap door and go down; 'down' recorded +25. Then take the art in the Gallery (+4).
4. After taking the art, go Gallery north to Studio and try 'up' at once; it led to Kitchen, then Living Room.
5. Once back in the Living Room with art, try 'put art in case' and check the score; this was untested here.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Do not cycle Cellar/East Chasm/Gallery/Studio; it cost most of the episode with zero new rooms or score. Pick an untried exit or 'look'.
2. Ignore the Maze 'take coins' goal; it was proposed about 20 times and never reached. Treat it as unreachable and explore elsewhere.
3. Do not spend moves on put down or take of lunch, pepper or manual; none of them scored.
4. After returning home, do not go back down the trap door without a new plan; explore untried above-ground exits first.
5. Avoid north from Cellar into the Troll room and never give items to the troll. The final 'north' from Gallery ended the episode at -10; cause unverified, so 'look' first.
